Understanding SSH message flow is critical for security analysis.\n\n## SSH Connection Phases\n\n### 1. Transport Layer (RFC 4253)\n- **Version Exchange**: Client and server exchange version strings\n- **Key Exchange**: Diffie-Hellman key exchange, algorithm negotiation\n- **Service Request**: Client requests services (ssh-userauth, ssh-connection)\n\n### 2. Authentication Layer (RFC 4252)\n- Must occur BEFORE any service execution\n- Methods: password, publickey, keyboard-interactive, hostbased\n- Message flow:\n  - `SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_REQUEST` - Client authentication attempt\n  - `SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_SUCCESS` - Authentication succeeded\n  - `SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_FAILURE` - Authentication failed\n\n### 3. Connection Layer (RFC 4254)\n- **Only available AFTER successful authentication**\n- Channel operations:\n  - `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN` - Open new channel\n  - `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST` - Request channel-specific operations\n  - `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_DATA` - Send data over channel\n\n## Critical SSH Message Types\n\n### Pre-Authentication Messages (Safe)\n- `SSH_MSG_DISCONNECT` (1)\n- `SSH_MSG_IGNORE` (2)\n- `SSH_MSG_UNIMPLEMENTED` (3)\n- `SSH_MSG_DEBUG` (4)\n- `SSH_MSG_SERVICE_REQUEST` (5)\n- `SSH_MSG_KEXINIT` (20)\n- `SSH_MSG_NEWKEYS` (21)\n\n### Post-Authentication Messages (DANGEROUS if allowed pre-auth)\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN` (90) - **CRITICAL**: Must check authentication\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN_CONFIRMATION` (91)\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN_FAILURE` (92)\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_WINDOW_ADJUST` (93)\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_DATA` (94)\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_EXTENDED_DATA` (95)\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_EOF` (96)\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_CLOSE` (97)\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST` (98) - **CRITICAL**: Can execute commands\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_SUCCESS` (99)\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_FAILURE` (100)\n\n## Common Vulnerabilities\n\n### Authentication Bypass\n**Pattern**: Server processes connection-layer messages before authentication completes\n\n**Attack Vector**:\n```\n1. Client connects\n2. Completes key exchange\n3. Sends SSH_MSG_SERVICE_REQUEST for \"ssh-connection\" (bypassing \"ssh-userauth\")\n4. Sends SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN to open session\n5. Sends SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST with \"exec\" to run commands\n```\n\n**Prevention**:\n- Always verify authentication state before processing connection messages\n- Reject `ssh-connection` service requests if authentication not completed\n- State machine must enforce: Transport → Auth → Connection\n\n### Message Type Validation\n```erlang\n% VULNERABLE CODE:\nhandle_message(Type, Data, State) ->\n    process_message(Type, Data, State).  % No auth check!\n\n% SECURE CODE:\nhandle_message(Type, Data, #state{authenticated = true} = State)\n    when Type >= 90 andalso Type =< 100 ->\n    process_message(Type, Data, State);\nhandle_message(Type, Data, #state{authenticated = false} = State)\n    when Type >= 90 ->\n    {disconnect, ?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR, State}.\n```\n\n## References\n- RFC 4251: SSH Protocol Architecture\n- RFC 4252: SSH Authentication Protocol\n- RFC 4253: SSH Transport Layer Protocol\n- RFC 4254: SSH Connection Protocol\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":362,"cache_read_input_tokens":18790,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":362,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f15156db-8dc6-4cd1-ba52-5e7280769252","uuid":"721f7759-8139-4d65-9a44-1493e2b5bc6b"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_vrtx_018tb7j6vPbDxxZQ7yZZGmT6","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_0145EwTYTmmw7FVbLsUz4Rtt","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/app/workspace/environment/skills/erlang-ssh-security-patterns.md","content":"# Erlang/OTP SSH Security Patterns\n\n## Overview\nErlang/OTP includes a built-in SSH server (`ssh` application). Understanding its architecture is critical for security analysis and vulnerability remediation.\n\n## Core SSH Modules\n\n### ssh_connection.erl\n- Handles SSH connection protocol (RFC 4254)\n- Manages channels, data transfer, and requests\n- **Security Critical**: Must validate authentication before processing\n\n### ssh_auth.erl / ssh_connection_handler.erl\n- Manages authentication state\n- Handles state transitions: init → kex → auth → connected\n- **Key Security Check**: Authentication state tracking\n\n### ssh_channel.erl\n- Implements channel behavior\n- Used for session, exec, subsystem channels\n\n## State Machine Architecture\n\n```erlang\n-record(state, {\n    authenticated = false :: boolean(),\n    auth_user = undefined :: undefined | binary(),\n    connection_state = init :: init | kex | auth | connected,\n    channels = #{} :: map(),\n    ...\n}).\n```\n\n## Critical Security Checks\n\n### 1. Authentication State Validation\n\n**Correct Pattern**:\n```erlang\nhandle_message(Type, Data, #state{authenticated = true} = State)\n    when Type >= ?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN ->\n    % Safe to process connection messages\n    process_channel_message(Type, Data, State);\n\nhandle_message(Type, Data, #state{authenticated = false} = State)\n    when Type >= ?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN ->\n    % Reject pre-authentication connection messages\n    disconnect(?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR,\n              \"Connection message before authentication\", State).\n```\n\n**Vulnerable Pattern**:\n```erlang\nhandle_message(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data, State) ->\n    % MISSING: authentication check!\n    open_channel(Data, State).\n```\n\n### 2. Service Request Validation\n\n**Correct Pattern**:\n```erlang\nhandle_service_request(\"ssh-userauth\", State) ->\n    % Always allow authentication service\n    {ok, State#state{service = \"ssh-userauth\"}};\n\nhandle_service_request(\"ssh-connection\", #state{authenticated = true} = State) ->\n    % Only allow after authentication\n    {ok, State#state{service = \"ssh-connection\"}};\n\nhandle_service_request(\"ssh-connection\", #state{authenticated = false} = State) ->\n    % Reject if not authenticated\n    {disconnect, ?SSH_DISCONNECT_SERVICE_NOT_AVAILABLE, State}.\n```\n\n### 3. Message Type Guards\n\n```erlang\n% Define message ranges\n-define(SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_FIRST, 50).\n-define(SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_LAST, 79).\n-define(SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_FIRST, 90).\n-define(SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_LAST, 100).\n\n% Guard for pre-auth messages\nis_pre_auth_allowed(Type) when Type =< 49 -> true;\nis_pre_auth_allowed(Type) when Type >= 50, Type =< 79 -> true; % auth messages\nis_pre_auth_allowed(_) -> false.\n\n% Guard for post-auth messages\nrequires_authentication(Type) when Type >= 90, Type =< 100 -> true;\nrequires_authentication(_) -> false.\n```\n\n## Common Vulnerability Patterns in Erlang SSH\n\n### 1. Missing Authentication Check\n```erlang\n% VULNERABLE\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data, State) ->\n    decode_and_open_channel(Data, State).\n\n% FIXED\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data, #state{authenticated = true} = State) ->\n    decode_and_open_channel(Data, State);\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, _, State) ->\n    {disconnect, ?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR, State}.\n```\n\n### 2. Permissive Service Acceptance\n```erlang\n% VULNERABLE\nhandle_service_request(Service, State) ->\n    {ok, State#state{service = Service}}.  % Accepts any service!\n\n% FIXED\nhandle_service_request(\"ssh-userauth\", State) ->\n    {ok, State#state{service = \"ssh-userauth\"}};\nhandle_service_request(\"ssh-connection\", #state{authenticated = true} = State) ->\n    {ok, State#state{service = \"ssh-connection\"}};\nhandle_service_request(_, State) ->\n    {disconnect, ?SSH_DISCONNECT_SERVICE_NOT_AVAILABLE, State}.\n```\n\n## Testing Security Fixes\n\n### Verify Authentication Enforcement\n```bash\n# Test 1: Try channel open before auth (should fail)\n# Send: SSH_MSG_SERVICE_REQUEST(\"ssh-connection\")\n# Send: SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN\n# Expected: Connection rejected or disconnected\n\n# Test 2: Normal auth flow (should work)\n# Send: SSH_MSG_SERVICE_REQUEST(\"ssh-userauth\")\n# Send: SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_REQUEST (with valid credentials)\n# Receive: SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_SUCCESS\n# Send: SSH_MSG_SERVICE_REQUEST(\"ssh-connection\")\n# Send: SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN\n# Expected: Channel opened successfully\n```\n\n## References\n- Erlang/OTP SSH Documentation\n- `ssh/src/ssh_connection.erl` - Connection handling\n- `ssh/src/ssh_connection_handler.erl` - Main state machine\n- `ssh/src/ssh_auth.erl` - Authentication logic\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":362,"cache_read_input_tokens":18790,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":362,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f15156db-8dc6-4cd1-ba52-5e7280769252","uuid":"78c56b5a-4b2f-495b-ad2c-6feb0ca23a0b"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_vrtx_018tb7j6vPbDxxZQ7yZZGmT6","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_015h1bPcyTgPcHFFBtdNZu7Z","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/app/workspace/environment/skills/vulnerability-analysis-methodology.md","content":"# Vulnerability Analysis Methodology\n\n## Overview\nSystematic approach to identifying and fixing security vulnerabilities in authentication systems, specifically pre-authentication command execution vulnerabilities.\n\n## Phase 1: Understanding the Vulnerability\n\n### Authentication Bypass Characteristics\n- **Type**: Pre-authentication command execution\n- **Impact**: Remote Code Execution (RCE) without credentials\n- **Root Cause**: Missing authentication state validation\n- **Attack Vector**: Crafted SSH protocol messages\n\n### Key Questions to Answer\n1. Which SSH message types can trigger command execution?\n2. Where is authentication state checked (or not checked)?\n3. What is the normal message flow vs. attack flow?\n4. Which functions process potentially dangerous messages?\n\n## Phase 2: Code Analysis Strategy\n\n### 1. Identify Entry Points\n```bash\n# Find message handlers\ngrep -r \"SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN\\|SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST\" --include=\"*.erl\"\n\n# Find service request handlers\ngrep -r \"ssh-connection\\|ssh-userauth\" --include=\"*.erl\"\n\n# Find authentication state checks\ngrep -r \"authenticated\" --include=\"*.erl\"\n```\n\n### 2. Trace Message Flow\n```\nClient → Network → ssh_transport → ssh_connection_handler → ssh_connection\n                                                          ↓\n                                                   Is authenticated?\n                                                   YES → Process\n                                                   NO → REJECT (if properly implemented)\n```\n\n### 3. Look for Missing Guards\n```erlang\n% VULNERABLE PATTERN - No authentication guard\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data, State) ->\n    ...\n\n% SECURE PATTERN - Authentication guard present\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data, #state{authenticated = true} = State) ->\n    ...\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, _, State) ->\n    {disconnect, ...}\n```\n\n## Phase 3: Vulnerability Confirmation\n\n### Indicators of Vulnerability\n\n1. **Missing Authentication Checks**\n   - Connection messages processed without checking `authenticated` flag\n   - Service requests accepted without validation\n\n2. **State Machine Flaws**\n   - Ability to skip authentication phase\n   - No enforcement of phase ordering (transport → auth → connection)\n\n3. **Permissive Message Handling**\n   - All messages accepted in all states\n   - No message type filtering based on connection state\n\n### Code Patterns to Search For\n\n```erlang\n% Pattern 1: Direct message processing without auth check\nhandle_message(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, ..., State) ->\n    % If no pattern match on 'authenticated' field, likely vulnerable\n    open_channel(...)\n\n% Pattern 2: Service request without validation\nhandle_service_request(Service, State) ->\n    State#state{service = Service}  % Accepts any service!\n\n% Pattern 3: Missing message type guards\nhandle_msg(Type, Data, State) when Type > 0 ->  % Too permissive!\n    process(Type, Data, State)\n```\n\n## Phase 4: Fix Implementation\n\n### Fix Strategy\n\n1. **Add Authentication Guards**\n   - Pattern match on `authenticated = true` for post-auth messages\n   - Explicitly reject post-auth messages when not authenticated\n\n2. **Validate Service Requests**\n   - Only allow \"ssh-connection\" service after authentication\n   - Always allow \"ssh-userauth\" service\n\n3. **Implement Defense in Depth**\n   - Check at multiple layers (service level, message level)\n   - Fail closed (reject by default)\n\n### Example Fix\n\n```erlang\n% BEFORE (Vulnerable)\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data, State) ->\n    open_channel(Data, State).\n\n% AFTER (Fixed)\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data, #state{authenticated = true} = State) ->\n    open_channel(Data, State);\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, _, State) ->\n    Msg = \"Channel operations not allowed before authentication\",\n    {disconnect, ?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR, Msg, State}.\n\n% Also fix service request handling\nhandle_service_request(\"ssh-connection\", #state{authenticated = false} = State) ->\n    {disconnect, ?SSH_DISCONNECT_SERVICE_NOT_AVAILABLE,\n     \"Authentication required\", State};\nhandle_service_request(\"ssh-connection\", #state{authenticated = true} = State) ->\n    {ok, State#state{service = \"ssh-connection\"}}.\n```\n\n## Phase 5: Verification\n\n### Test Cases\n\n1. **Normal Flow (Should Work)**\n   - Connect → Key Exchange → Auth Service → Authenticate → Connection Service → Execute Command\n\n2. **Attack Flow (Should Fail)**\n   - Connect → Key Exchange → Connection Service (NO AUTH) → Execute Command\n   - Expected: Disconnected with protocol error\n\n3. **Edge Cases**\n   - Multiple service requests\n   - Out-of-order messages\n   - Malformed messages\n\n### Validation Checklist\n- [ ] Connection messages rejected before authentication\n- [ ] \"ssh-connection\" service rejected before authentication\n- [ ] Normal authentication flow still works\n- [ ] Commands can execute after successful authentication\n- [ ] No regression in other SSH functionality\n\n## Tools and Techniques\n\n### Static Analysis\n```bash\n# Find all message handlers\ngrep -E \"handle_(msg|message)\" --include=\"*.erl\" -r .\n\n# Find authentication state usage\ngrep -E \"authenticated\\s*=\" --include=\"*.erl\" -r .\n\n# Find service handling\ngrep -E \"service.*request\" --include=\"*.erl\" -i -r .\n```\n\n### Code Review Focus Areas\n1. Message dispatching logic\n2. State machine transitions\n3. Authentication flag management\n4. Service request handling\n5. Channel operation functions\n\n## References\n- OWASP Authentication Bypass\n- CWE-306: Missing Authentication for Critical Function\n- CVE patterns for SSH authentication bypass vulnerabilities\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":362,"cache_read_input_tokens":18790,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":362,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f15156db-8dc6-4cd1-ba52-5e7280769252","uuid":"6f62f760-afd5-492d-8b0c-b833f41fbf73"}

Connection Layer (RFC 4254)\n- **Only available AFTER successful authentication**\n- Channel operations:\n  - `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN` - Open new channel\n  - `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST` - Request channel-specific operations\n  - `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_DATA` - Send data over channel\n\n## Critical SSH Message Types\n\n### Pre-Authentication Messages (Safe)\n- `SSH_MSG_DISCONNECT` (1)\n- `SSH_MSG_IGNORE` (2)\n- `SSH_MSG_UNIMPLEMENTED` (3)\n- `SSH_MSG_DEBUG` (4)\n- `SSH_MSG_SERVICE_REQUEST` (5)\n- `SSH_MSG_KEXINIT` (20)\n- `SSH_MSG_NEWKEYS` (21)\n\n### Post-Authentication Messages (DANGEROUS if allowed pre-auth)\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN` (90) - **CRITICAL**: Must check authentication\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN_CONFIRMATION` (91)\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN_FAILURE` (92)\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_WINDOW_ADJUST` (93)\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_DATA` (94)\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_EXTENDED_DATA` (95)\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_EOF` (96)\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_CLOSE` (97)\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST` (98) - **CRITICAL**: Can execute commands\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_SUCCESS` (99)\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_FAILURE` (100)\n\n## Common Vulnerabilities\n\n### Authentication Bypass\n**Pattern**: Server processes connection-layer messages before authentication completes\n\n**Attack Vector**:\n```\n1. Client connects\n2. Completes key exchange\n3. Sends SSH_MSG_SERVICE_REQUEST for \"ssh-connection\" (bypassing \"ssh-userauth\")\n4. Sends SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN to open session\n5. Sends SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST with \"exec\" to run commands\n```\n\n**Prevention**:\n- Always verify authentication state before processing connection messages\n- Reject `ssh-connection` service requests if authentication not completed\n- State machine must enforce: Transport → Auth → Connection\n\n### Message Type Validation\n```erlang\n% VULNERABLE CODE:\nhandle_message(Type, Data, State) ->\n    process_message(Type, Data, State).  % No auth check!\n\n% SECURE CODE:\nhandle_message(Type, Data, #state{authenticated = true} = State)\n    when Type >= 90 andalso Type =< 100 ->\n    process_message(Type, Data, State);\nhandle_message(Type, Data, #state{authenticated = false} = State)\n    when Type >= 90 ->\n    {disconnect, ?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR, State}.\n```\n\n## References\n- RFC 4251: SSH Protocol Architecture\n- RFC 4252: SSH Authentication Protocol\n- RFC 4253: SSH Transport Layer Protocol\n- RFC 4254: SSH Connection Protocol\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Understanding its architecture is critical for security analysis and vulnerability remediation.\n\n## Core SSH Modules\n\n### ssh_connection.erl\n- Handles SSH connection protocol (RFC 4254)\n- Manages channels, data transfer, and requests\n- **Security Critical**: Must validate authentication before processing\n\n### ssh_auth.erl / ssh_connection_handler.erl\n- Manages authentication state\n- Handles state transitions: init → kex → auth → connected\n- **Key Security Check**: Authentication state tracking\n\n### ssh_channel.erl\n- Implements channel behavior\n- Used for session, exec, subsystem channels\n\n## State Machine Architecture\n\n```erlang\n-record(state, {\n    authenticated = false :: boolean(),\n    auth_user = undefined :: undefined | binary(),\n    connection_state = init :: init | kex | auth | connected,\n    channels = #{} :: map(),\n    ...\n}).\n```\n\n## Critical Security Checks\n\n### 1. Authentication State Validation\n\n**Correct Pattern**:\n```erlang\nhandle_message(Type, Data, #state{authenticated = true} = State)\n    when Type >= ?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN ->\n    % Safe to process connection messages\n    process_channel_message(Type, Data, State);\n\nhandle_message(Type, Data, #state{authenticated = false} = State)\n    when Type >= ?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN ->\n    % Reject pre-authentication connection messages\n    disconnect(?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR,\n              \"Connection message before authentication\", State).\n```\n\n**Vulnerable Pattern**:\n```erlang\nhandle_message(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data, State) ->\n    % MISSING: authentication check!\n    open_channel(Data, State).\n```\n\n### 2. Service Request Validation\n\n**Correct Pattern**:\n```erlang\nhandle_service_request(\"ssh-userauth\", State) ->\n    % Always allow authentication service\n    {ok, State#state{service = \"ssh-userauth\"}};\n\nhandle_service_request(\"ssh-connection\", #state{authenticated = true} = State) ->\n    % Only allow after authentication\n    {ok, State#state{service = \"ssh-connection\"}};\n\nhandle_service_request(\"ssh-connection\", #state{authenticated = false} = State) ->\n    % Reject if not authenticated\n    {disconnect, ?SSH_DISCONNECT_SERVICE_NOT_AVAILABLE, State}.\n```\n\n### 3. Message Type Guards\n\n```erlang\n% Define message ranges\n-define(SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_FIRST, 50).\n-define(SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_LAST, 79).\n-define(SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_FIRST, 90).\n-define(SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_LAST, 100).\n\n% Guard for pre-auth messages\nis_pre_auth_allowed(Type) when Type =< 49 -> true;\nis_pre_auth_allowed(Type) when Type >= 50, Type =< 79 -> true; % auth messages\nis_pre_auth_allowed(_) -> false.\n\n% Guard for post-auth messages\nrequires_authentication(Type) when Type >= 90, Type =< 100 -> true;\nrequires_authentication(_) -> false.\n```\n\n## Common Vulnerability Patterns in Erlang SSH\n\n### 1. Missing Authentication Check\n```erlang\n% VULNERABLE\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data, State) ->\n    decode_and_open_channel(Data, State).\n\n% FIXED\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data, #state{authenticated = true} = State) ->\n    decode_and_open_channel(Data, State);\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, _, State) ->\n    {disconnect, ?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR, State}.\n```\n\n### 2. Permissive Service Acceptance\n```erlang\n% VULNERABLE\nhandle_service_request(Service, State) ->\n    {ok, State#state{service = Service}}.  % Accepts any service!\n\n% FIXED\nhandle_service_request(\"ssh-userauth\", State) ->\n    {ok, State#state{service = \"ssh-userauth\"}};\nhandle_service_request(\"ssh-connection\", #state{authenticated = true} = State) ->\n    {ok, State#state{service = \"ssh-connection\"}};\nhandle_service_request(_, State) ->\n    {disconnect, ?SSH_DISCONNECT_SERVICE_NOT_AVAILABLE, State}.\n```\n\n## Testing Security Fixes\n\n### Verify Authentication Enforcement\n```bash\n# Test 1: Try channel open before auth (should fail)\n# Send: SSH_MSG_SERVICE_REQUEST(\"ssh-connection\")\n# Send: SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN\n# Expected: Connection rejected or disconnected\n\n# Test 2: Normal auth flow (should work)\n# Send: SSH_MSG_SERVICE_REQUEST(\"ssh-userauth\")\n# Send: SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_REQUEST (with valid credentials)\n# Receive: SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_SUCCESS\n# Send: SSH_MSG_SERVICE_REQUEST(\"ssh-connection\")\n# Send: SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN\n# Expected: Channel opened successfully\n```\n\n## References\n- Erlang/OTP SSH Documentation\n- `ssh/src/ssh_connection.erl` - Connection handling\n- `ssh/src/ssh_connection_handler.erl` - Main state machine\n- `ssh/src/ssh_auth.erl` - Authentication logic\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Which SSH message types can trigger command execution?\n2. Where is authentication state checked (or not checked)?\n3. What is the normal message flow vs. attack flow?\n4. Which functions process potentially dangerous messages?\n\n## Phase 2: Code Analysis Strategy\n\n### 1. Identify Entry Points\n```bash\n# Find message handlers\ngrep -r \"SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN\\|SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST\" --include=\"*.erl\"\n\n# Find service request handlers\ngrep -r \"ssh-connection\\|ssh-userauth\" --include=\"*.erl\"\n\n# Find authentication state checks\ngrep -r \"authenticated\" --include=\"*.erl\"\n```\n\n### 2. Trace Message Flow\n```\nClient → Network → ssh_transport → ssh_connection_handler → ssh_connection\n                                                          ↓\n                                                   Is authenticated?\n                                                   YES → Process\n                                                   NO → REJECT (if properly implemented)\n```\n\n### 3. Look for Missing Guards\n```erlang\n% VULNERABLE PATTERN - No authentication guard\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data, State) ->\n    ...\n\n% SECURE PATTERN - Authentication guard present\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data, #state{authenticated = true} = State) ->\n    ...\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, _, State) ->\n    {disconnect, ...}\n```\n\n## Phase 3: Vulnerability Confirmation\n\n### Indicators of Vulnerability\n\n1. **Missing Authentication Checks**\n   - Connection messages processed without checking `authenticated` flag\n   - Service requests accepted without validation\n\n2. **State Machine Flaws**\n   - Ability to skip authentication phase\n   - No enforcement of phase ordering (transport → auth → connection)\n\n3. **Permissive Message Handling**\n   - All messages accepted in all states\n   - No message type filtering based on connection state\n\n### Code Patterns to Search For\n\n```erlang\n% Pattern 1: Direct message processing without auth check\nhandle_message(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, ..., State) ->\n    % If no pattern match on 'authenticated' field, likely vulnerable\n    open_channel(...)\n\n% Pattern 2: Service request without validation\nhandle_service_request(Service, State) ->\n    State#state{service = Service}  % Accepts any service!\n\n% Pattern 3: Missing message type guards\nhandle_msg(Type, Data, State) when Type > 0 ->  % Too permissive!\n    process(Type, Data, State)\n```\n\n## Phase 4: Fix Implementation\n\n### Fix Strategy\n\n1. **Add Authentication Guards**\n   - Pattern match on `authenticated = true` for post-auth messages\n   - Explicitly reject post-auth messages when not authenticated\n\n2. **Validate Service Requests**\n   - Only allow \"ssh-connection\" service after authentication\n   - Always allow \"ssh-userauth\" service\n\n3. **Implement Defense in Depth**\n   - Check at multiple layers (service level, message level)\n   - Fail closed (reject by default)\n\n### Example Fix\n\n```erlang\n% BEFORE (Vulnerable)\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data, State) ->\n    open_channel(Data, State).\n\n% AFTER (Fixed)\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data, #state{authenticated = true} = State) ->\n    open_channel(Data, State);\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, _, State) ->\n    Msg = \"Channel operations not allowed before authentication\",\n    {disconnect, ?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR, Msg, State}.\n\n% Also fix service request handling\nhandle_service_request(\"ssh-connection\", #state{authenticated = false} = State) ->\n    {disconnect, ?SSH_DISCONNECT_SERVICE_NOT_AVAILABLE,\n     \"Authentication required\", State};\nhandle_service_request(\"ssh-connection\", #state{authenticated = true} = State) ->\n    {ok, State#state{service = \"ssh-connection\"}}.\n```\n\n## Phase 5: Verification\n\n### Test Cases\n\n1. **Normal Flow (Should Work)**\n   - Connect → Key Exchange → Auth Service → Authenticate → Connection Service → Execute Command\n\n2. **Attack Flow (Should Fail)**\n   - Connect → Key Exchange → Connection Service (NO AUTH) → Execute Command\n   - Expected: Disconnected with protocol error\n\n3. **Edge Cases**\n   - Multiple service requests\n   - Out-of-order messages\n   - Malformed messages\n\n### Validation Checklist\n- [ ] Connection messages rejected before authentication\n- [ ] \"ssh-connection\" service rejected before authentication\n- [ ] Normal authentication flow still works\n- [ ] Commands can execute after successful authentication\n- [ ] No regression in other SSH functionality\n\n## Tools and Techniques\n\n### Static Analysis\n```bash\n# Find all message handlers\ngrep -E \"handle_(msg|message)\" --include=\"*.erl\" -r .\n\n# Find authentication state usage\ngrep -E \"authenticated\\s*=\" --include=\"*.erl\" -r .\n\n# Find service handling\ngrep -E \"service.*request\" --include=\"*.erl\" -i -r .\n```\n\n### Code Review Focus Areas\n1. Message dispatching logic\n2. State machine transitions\n3. Authentication flag management\n4. Service request handling\n5. Channel operation functions\n\n## References\n- OWASP Authentication Bypass\n- CWE-306: Missing Authentication for Critical Function\n- CVE patterns for SSH authentication bypass vulnerabilities\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Proper use of guards and pattern matching can prevent entire classes of vulnerabilities.\n\n## Security Through Pattern Matching\n\n### 1. State-Based Access Control\n\n**Principle**: Use pattern matching to enforce state requirements\n\n```erlang\n% Only allow operation when authenticated\nhandle_operation(Data, #state{authenticated = true} = State) ->\n    perform_operation(Data, State);\n\n% Explicitly reject when not authenticated\nhandle_operation(_, #state{authenticated = false} = State) ->\n    {error, not_authenticated, State}.\n```\n\n**Why This Works**:\n- Pattern matching happens at function clause selection\n- No code path exists to perform operation without authentication\n- Compiler enforces the constraint\n\n### 2. Message Type Guards\n\n```erlang\n-define(SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_REQUEST, 50).\n-define(SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, 90).\n-define(SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST, 98).\n\n% Pre-authentication: Only allow specific message types\nhandle_msg(Type, Data, #state{authenticated = false} = State)\n    when Type =< 49; Type >= 50, Type =< 79 ->\n    % Transport and auth messages only\n    process_pre_auth_msg(Type, Data, State);\n\n% Pre-authentication: Reject connection messages\nhandle_msg(Type, _, #state{authenticated = false} = State)\n    when Type >= 90 ->\n    {disconnect, ?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR, State};\n\n% Post-authentication: Allow all messages\nhandle_msg(Type, Data, #state{authenticated = true} = State) ->\n    process_msg(Type, Data, State).\n```\n\n### 3. Multi-Layer Defense\n\n```erlang\n% Layer 1: Service request validation\nrequest_service(\"ssh-userauth\", State) ->\n    {ok, State#state{service = \"ssh-userauth\"}};\n\nrequest_service(\"ssh-connection\", #state{authenticated = true} = State) ->\n    {ok, State#state{service = \"ssh-connection\"}};\n\nrequest_service(\"ssh-connection\", #state{authenticated = false}) ->\n    {error, authentication_required};\n\nrequest_service(_, _) ->\n    {error, unknown_service}.\n\n% Layer 2: Message validation\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data,\n           #state{service = \"ssh-connection\", authenticated = true} = State) ->\n    open_channel(Data, State);\n\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, _, State) ->\n    {disconnect, ?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR, State}.\n```\n\n## Common Security Anti-Patterns\n\n### Anti-Pattern 1: Runtime Checks Instead of Pattern Matching\n\n```erlang\n% BAD: Runtime check (can be bypassed or forgotten)\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data, State) ->\n    case State#state.authenticated of\n        true -> open_channel(Data, State);\n        false -> {error, not_authenticated}\n    end.\n\n% GOOD: Pattern matching (enforced by compiler)\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data, #state{authenticated = true} = State) ->\n    open_channel(Data, State);\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, _, State) ->\n    {error, not_authenticated, State}.\n```\n\n### Anti-Pattern 2: Catch-All Clauses\n\n```erlang\n% BAD: Catch-all without authentication check\nhandle_msg(Type, Data, State) ->\n    process_message(Type, Data, State).  % Accepts everything!\n\n% GOOD: Explicit handling with security checks\nhandle_msg(Type, Data, #state{authenticated = true} = State)\n    when Type >= 90, Type =< 100 ->\n    process_connection_msg(Type, Data, State);\nhandle_msg(Type, Data, State) when Type =< 49 ->\n    process_transport_msg(Type, Data, State);\nhandle_msg(Type, _, State) ->\n    {disconnect, ?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR, State}.\n```\n\n### Anti-Pattern 3: Missing Negative Cases\n\n```erlang\n% BAD: Only defines positive case\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data, #state{authenticated = true} = State) ->\n    open_channel(Data, State).\n% What happens if authenticated = false? Falls through to next clause or crashes!\n\n% GOOD: Explicitly handles both cases\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data, #state{authenticated = true} = State) ->\n    open_channel(Data, State);\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, _, State) ->\n    {disconnect, ?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR, State}.\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n### 1. Fail Closed\nAlways define the restrictive case first, permissive case second\n\n```erlang\n% Deny by default\nhandle_request(Type, _, #state{authenticated = false}) when Type >= 90 ->\n    {error, not_authenticated};\n\n% Allow when authenticated\nhandle_request(Type, Data, #state{authenticated = true}) when Type >= 90 ->\n    process(Type, Data).\n```\n\n### 2. Use Guards for Ranges\n\n```erlang\n% Good: Clear message type ranges\nis_transport_msg(Type) when Type >= 1, Type =< 49 -> true;\nis_transport_msg(_) -> false.\n\nis_auth_msg(Type) when Type >= 50, Type =< 79 -> true;\nis_auth_msg(_) -> false.\n\nis_connection_msg(Type) when Type >= 90, Type =< 100 -> true;\nis_connection_msg(_) -> false.\n```\n\n### 3. Combine State and Type Checks\n\n```erlang\n% Both state and message type must match\nhandle_msg(Type, Data, #state{authenticated = true, service = \"ssh-connection\"} = State)\n    when Type >= 90, Type =< 100 ->\n    process_connection_msg(Type, Data, State);\n\nhandle_msg(Type, _, State) when Type >= 90 ->\n    {disconnect, ?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR, State}.\n```\n\n## Testing Pattern Match Security\n\n### Test Case Structure\n\n```erlang\n% Test 1: Authenticated state allows operation\nState1 = #state{authenticated = true},\n{ok, _} = handle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data, State1).\n\n% Test 2: Unauthenticated state rejects operation\nState2 = #state{authenticated = false},\n{disconnect, _, _} = handle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data, State2).\n\n% Test 3: Wrong service rejects even if authenticated\nState3 = #state{authenticated = true, service = \"ssh-userauth\"},\n{error, _} = handle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data, State3).\n```\n\n## Pattern Matching Checklist\n\nWhen implementing security-critical functions:\n\n- [ ] Pattern match on security-relevant state fields (`authenticated`, `service`, etc.)\n- [ ] Use guards for message type ranges\n- [ ] Define both positive and negative cases explicitly\n- [ ] Order clauses from most restrictive to least restrictive\n- [ ] Avoid catch-all clauses that bypass security checks\n- [ ] Test both authorized and unauthorized access attempts\n\n## References\n- Erlang Pattern Matching: https://www.erlang.org/doc/reference_manual/patterns.html\n- Erlang Guards: https://www.erlang.org/doc/reference_manual/expressions.html#guards\n- Security by Construction #state{authenticated = false}) ->\n    {error, authentication_required};\n\nrequest_service(_, _) ->\n    {error, unknown_service}.\n\n% Layer 2: Message validation\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data,\n           #state{service = \"ssh-connection\", authenticated = true} = State) ->\n    open_channel(Data, State);\n\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, _, State) ->\n    {disconnect, ?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR, State}.\n```\n\n## Common Security Anti-Patterns\n\n### Anti-Pattern 1: Runtime Checks Instead of Pattern Matching\n\n```erlang\n% BAD: Runtime check (can be bypassed end.\n\n% GOOD: Pattern matching (enforced by compiler)\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data, #state{authenticated = true} = State) ->\n    open_channel(Data, State);\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, _, State) ->\n    {error, not_authenticated, State}.\n```\n\n### Anti-Pattern 2: Catch-All Clauses\n\n```erlang\n% BAD: Catch-all without authentication check\nhandle_msg(Type, Data, State) ->\n    process_message(Type, Data, State).  % Accepts everything!\n\n% GOOD: Explicit handling with security checks\nhandle_msg(Type, Data, #state{authenticated = true} = State)\n    when Type >= 90, Type =< 100 ->\n    process_connection_msg(Type, Data, State);\nhandle_msg(Type, Data, State) when Type =< 49 ->\n    process_transport_msg(Type, Data, State);\nhandle_msg(Type, _, State) ->\n    {disconnect, ?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR, State}.\n```\n\n### Anti-Pattern 3: Missing Negative Cases\n\n```erlang\n% BAD: Only defines positive case\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data, #state{authenticated = true} = State) ->\n    open_channel(Data, State).\n% What happens if authenticated = false? Falls through to next clause or crashes!\n\n% GOOD: Explicitly handles both cases\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data, #state{authenticated = true} = State) ->\n    open_channel(Data, State);\nhandle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, _, State) ->\n    {disconnect, ?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR, State}.\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n### 1. Fail Closed\nAlways define the restrictive case first, permissive case second\n\n```erlang\n% Deny by default\nhandle_request(Type, _, #state{authenticated = false}) when Type >= 90 ->\n    {error, not_authenticated};\n\n% Allow when authenticated\nhandle_request(Type, Data, #state{authenticated = true}) when Type >= 90 ->\n    process(Type, Data).\n```\n\n### 2. Use Guards for Ranges\n\n```erlang\n% Good: Clear message type ranges\nis_transport_msg(Type) when Type >= 1, Type =< 49 -> true;\nis_transport_msg(_) -> false.\n\nis_auth_msg(Type) when Type >= 50, Type =< 79 -> true;\nis_auth_msg(_) -> false.\n\nis_connection_msg(Type) when Type >= 90, Type =< 100 -> true;\nis_connection_msg(_) -> false.\n```\n\n### 3. Combine State and Type Checks\n\n```erlang\n% Both state and message type must match\nhandle_msg(Type, Data, #state{authenticated = true, service = \"ssh-connection\"} = State)\n    when Type >= 90, Type =< 100 ->\n    process_connection_msg(Type, Data, State);\n\nhandle_msg(Type, _, State) when Type >= 90 ->\n    {disconnect, ?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR, State}.\n```\n\n## Testing Pattern Match Security\n\n### Test Case Structure\n\n```erlang\n% Test 1: Authenticated state allows operation\nState1 = #state{authenticated = true},\n{ok, _} = handle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data, State1).\n\n% Test 2: Unauthenticated state rejects operation\nState2 = #state{authenticated = false},\n{disconnect, _, _} = handle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data, State2).\n\n% Test 3: Wrong service rejects even if authenticated\nState3 = #state{authenticated = true, service = \"ssh-userauth\"},\n{error, _} = handle_msg(?SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN, Data, State3).\n```\n\n## Pattern Matching Checklist\n\nWhen implementing security-critical functions:\n\n- [ ] Pattern match on security-relevant state fields (`authenticated`, `service`, etc.)\n- [ ] Use guards for message type ranges\n- [ ] Define both positive and negative cases explicitly\n- [ ] Order clauses from most restrictive to least restrictive\n- [ ] Avoid catch-all clauses that bypass security checks\n- [ ] Test both authorized and unauthorized access attempts\n\n## References\n- Erlang Pattern Matching: https://www.erlang.org/doc/reference_manual/patterns.html\n- Erlang Guards: https://www.erlang.org/doc/reference_manual/expressions.html#guards\n- Security by Construction principles\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

License.\n%%\n%% %CopyrightEnd%\n%%\n%%\n%%----------------------------------------------------------------------\n%% Purpose: Handles an ssh connection, e.i. both the\n%% setup SSH Transport Layer Protocol (RFC 4253), Authentication\n%% Protocol (RFC 4252) and SSH connection Protocol (RFC 4255)\n%% Details of the different protocols are\n%% implemented in ssh_transport.erl, ssh_auth.erl and ssh_connection.erl\n%% ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n-module(ssh_connection_handler).\n-moduledoc false.\n\n-behaviour(gen_statem).\n\n-include(\"ssh.hrl\").\n-include(\"ssh_transport.hrl\").\n-include(\"ssh_auth.hrl\").\n-include(\"ssh_connect.hrl\").\n\n-include(\"ssh_fsm.hrl\").\n\n%%====================================================================\n%%% Exports\n%%====================================================================\n\n%%% Start and stop\n-export([start_link/3, start_link/4,\n         takeover/4,\n\t stop/1\n\t]).\n\n%%% Internal application API\n-export([available_hkey_algorithms/2,\n\t open_channel/6,\n         start_channel/5,\n         handshake/2,\n         handle_direct_tcpip/6,\n\t request/6, request/7,\n\t reply_request/3,\n         global_request/5,\n         handle_ssh_msg_ext_info/2,\n\t send/5,\n         send_bytes/2,\n         send_msg/2,\n\t send_eof/2,\n         send_disconnect/6,\n         send_disconnect/7,\n         store/3,\n         retrieve/2,\n\t info/1, info/2,\n\t connection_info/2,\n\t channel_info/3,\n\t adjust_window/3, close/2,\n\t disconnect/4,\n\t get_print_info/1,\n         set_sock_opts/2, get_sock_opts/2,\n         prohibited_sock_option/1\n\t]).\n\n%%% Behaviour callbacks\n-export([init/1, callback_mode/0, handle_event/4, terminate/3,\n\t format_status/2, code_change/4]).\n\n%%% Exports not intended to be used :). They are used for spawning and tests\n-export([init_ssh_record/3,\t\t   % Export of this internal function\n\t\t\t\t\t   % intended for low-level protocol test suites\n\t renegotiate/1, alg/1 % Export intended for test cases\n\t]).\n\n-behaviour(ssh_dbg).\n-export([ssh_dbg_trace_points/0, ssh_dbg_flags/1,\n         ssh_dbg_on/1, ssh_dbg_off/1,\n         ssh_dbg_format/2, ssh_dbg_format/3]).\n\n\n-define(call_disconnectfun_and_log_cond(LogMsg, DetailedText, StateName, D),\n        call_disconnectfun_and_log_cond(LogMsg, DetailedText, ?MODULE, ?LINE, StateName, D)).\n\n%%====================================================================\n%% Start / stop\n%%====================================================================\n\nstart_link(Role, Socket, Options) ->\n    start_link(Role, undefined, Socket, Options).\n\nstart_link(Role, Id, Socket, Options) ->\n    case gen_statem:start_link(?MODULE,\n                               [Role, Socket, Options],\n                               [{spawn_opt, [{message_queue_data,off_heap}]}]) of\n\n        {ok, Pid} when Id =/= undefined ->\n            %% Announce the ConnectionRef to the system supervisor so it could\n            %%   1) initiate the socket handover, and\n            %%   2) be returned to whoever called for example ssh:connect; the Pid\n            %%      returned from this function is \"consumed\" by the connection\n            %%      supervisor.\n            ?GET_INTERNAL_OPT(user_pid,Options) ! {new_connection_ref, Id, Pid},\n            {ok, Pid};\n\n        Others ->\n            Others\n    end.\n\ntakeover(ConnPid, Role, Socket, Options) ->\n    case Role of\n        client ->\n            group_leader(group_leader(), ConnPid);\n        _ ->\n            ok\n    end,\n    {_, Callback, _} = ?GET_OPT(transport, Options),\n    case Callback:controlling_process(Socket, ConnPid) of\n        ok ->\n            Ref = erlang:monitor(process, ConnPid),\n            gen_statem:cast(ConnPid, socket_control),\n            NegTimeout = ?GET_INTERNAL_OPT(negotiation_timeout,\n                                           Options,\n                                           ?GET_OPT(negotiation_timeout, Options)\n                                          ),\n            handshake(ConnPid, Role, Ref, NegTimeout);\n        {error, Reason}\t->\n            {error, Reason}\n    end.\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n-spec stop(connection_ref()\n\t  ) -> ok | {error, term()}.\n%% . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .\nstop(ConnectionHandler)->\n    case call(ConnectionHandler, stop) of\n       {error, closed} ->\n\t    ok;\n\tOther ->\n\t    Other\n    end.\n\n%%====================================================================\n%% Internal application API\n%%====================================================================\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n%%% Some other module has decided to disconnect.\n\n-spec disconnect(Code::integer(), Details::iodata(),\n                      Module::atom(), Line::integer()) -> no_return().\n%% . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .\n\n% Preferable called with the macro ?DISCONNECT\n\ndisconnect(Code, DetailedText, Module, Line) ->\n    throw({keep_state_and_data,\n\t   [{next_event, internal, {send_disconnect, Code, DetailedText, Module, Line}}]}).\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n%%% Open a channel in the connection to the peer, that is, do the ssh\n%%% signalling with the peer.\n-spec open_channel(connection_ref(), \n\t\t   string(),\n\t\t   iodata(),\n\t\t   pos_integer() | undefined,\n\t\t   pos_integer() | undefined,\n\t\t   timeout()\n\t\t  ) -> {open, channel_id()} | {error, term()}.\n\n%% . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .\nopen_channel(ConnectionHandler,\n\t     ChannelType, ChannelSpecificData, InitialWindowSize, MaxPacketSize, \n\t     Timeout) ->\n    call(ConnectionHandler,\n\t {open,\n\t  self(),\n\t  ChannelType, InitialWindowSize, MaxPacketSize, ChannelSpecificData,\n\t  Timeout}).\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n%%% Start a channel handling process in the superviser tree\n-spec start_channel(connection_ref(), atom(), channel_id(), list(), term()) ->\n                           {ok, pid()} | {error, term()}.\n\n%% . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .\nstart_channel(ConnectionHandler, CallbackModule, ChannelId, Args, Exec) ->\n    {ok, {ConnectionSup,Role,Opts}} = call(ConnectionHandler, get_misc),\n    ssh_connection_sup:start_channel(Role, ConnectionSup,\n                                    ConnectionHandler, CallbackModule, ChannelId,\n                                    Args, Exec, Opts).\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\nhandle_direct_tcpip(ConnectionHandler, ListenHost, ListenPort, ConnectToHost, ConnectToPort, Timeout) ->\n    call(ConnectionHandler, {handle_direct_tcpip, ListenHost, ListenPort, ConnectToHost, ConnectToPort, Timeout}).\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n-spec request(connection_ref(),\n\t      pid(),\n\t      channel_id(),\n\t      string(),\n\t      boolean(),\n\t      iodata(),\n\t      timeout()\n\t     ) -> success | failure | ok | {error,timeout}.\n\n-spec request(connection_ref(),\n\t      channel_id(),\n\t      string(),\n\t      boolean(),\n\t      iodata(),\n\t      timeout()\n\t     ) -> success | failure | ok | {error,timeout}.\n%% . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .\nrequest(ConnectionHandler, ChannelPid, ChannelId, Type, true, Data, Timeout) ->\n    call(ConnectionHandler, {request, ChannelPid, ChannelId, Type, Data, Timeout});\nrequest(ConnectionHandler, ChannelPid, ChannelId, Type, false, Data, _) ->\n    cast(ConnectionHandler, {request, ChannelPid, ChannelId, Type, Data}).\n\nrequest(ConnectionHandler, ChannelId, Type, true, Data, Timeout) ->\n    call(ConnectionHandler, {request, ChannelId, Type, Data, Timeout});\nrequest(ConnectionHandler, ChannelId, Type, false, Data, _) ->\n    cast(ConnectionHandler, {request, ChannelId, Type, Data}).\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n-spec reply_request(connection_ref(),\n\t\t    success | failure,\n\t\t    channel_id()\n\t\t   ) -> ok.\n\n%% . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .\nreply_request(ConnectionHandler, Status, ChannelId) ->\n    cast(ConnectionHandler, {reply_request, Status, ChannelId}).\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\nglobal_request(ConnectionHandler, Type, true, Data, Timeout) ->\n    call(ConnectionHandler, {global_request, Type, Data, Timeout});\nglobal_request(ConnectionHandler, Type, false, Data, _) ->\n    cast(ConnectionHandler, {global_request, Type, Data}).\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n-spec send(connection_ref(),\n\t   channel_id(),\n\t   non_neg_integer(),\n\t   iodata(),\n\t   timeout()\n\t  ) -> ok | {error, timeout|closed}.\n%% . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .\nsend(ConnectionHandler, ChannelId, Type, Data, Timeout) ->\n    call(ConnectionHandler, {data, ChannelId, Type, Data, Timeout}).\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n-spec send_eof(connection_ref(),\n\t       channel_id()\n\t      ) -> ok | {error,closed}.\n%% . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .\nsend_eof(ConnectionHandler, ChannelId) ->\n    call(ConnectionHandler, {eof, ChannelId}).\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n-spec info(connection_ref()\n\t  ) -> {ok, [#channel{}]} .\n\n-spec info(connection_ref(),\n\t   pid() | all\n\t  ) -> {ok, [#channel{}]} .\n%% . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .\ninfo(ConnectionHandler) ->\n    info(ConnectionHandler, all).\n\ninfo(ConnectionHandler, ChannelProcess) ->\n    call(ConnectionHandler, {info, ChannelProcess}).\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n-type local_sock_info() :: {inet:ip_address(), non_neg_integer()} | string().\n-type peer_sock_info()  :: {inet:ip_address(), non_neg_integer()} | string().\n-type state_info() :: iolist().\n\n-spec get_print_info(connection_ref()\n\t\t    ) -> {{local_sock_info(), peer_sock_info()},\n\t\t\t  state_info()\n\t\t\t }.\n%% . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .\nget_print_info(ConnectionHandler) ->\n    call(ConnectionHandler, get_print_info, 1000).\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\nconnection_info(ConnectionHandler, []) ->\n    connection_info(ConnectionHandler, conn_info_keys());\nconnection_info(ConnectionHandler, Key) when is_atom(Key) ->\n    case connection_info(ConnectionHandler, [Key]) of\n        [{Key,Val}] -> {Key,Val};\n        Other -> Other\n    end;\nconnection_info(ConnectionHandler, Options) ->\n    call(ConnectionHandler, {connection_info, Options}).\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n-spec channel_info(connection_ref(),\n\t\t   channel_id(),\n\t\t   [atom()]\n\t\t  ) -> proplists:proplist().\n%% . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .\nchannel_info(ConnectionHandler, ChannelId, Options) ->\n    call(ConnectionHandler, {channel_info, ChannelId, Options}).\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n-spec adjust_window(connection_ref(),\n\t\t    channel_id(),\n\t\t    integer()\n\t\t   ) -> ok.\n%% . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .\nadjust_window(ConnectionHandler, Channel, Bytes) ->\n    cast(ConnectionHandler, {adjust_window, Channel, Bytes}).\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n-spec close(connection_ref(),\n\t    channel_id()\n\t   ) -> ok.\n%% . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .\nclose(ConnectionHandler, ChannelId) ->\n    case call(ConnectionHandler, {close, ChannelId}) of\n\tok ->\n\t    ok;\n\t{error, closed} ->\n\t    ok\n    end.\n\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\nstore(ConnectionHandler, Key, Value) ->\n    cast(ConnectionHandler, {store,Key,Value}).\n\nretrieve(#connection{options=Opts}, Key) ->\n    try ?GET_INTERNAL_OPT(Key, Opts) of\n        Value ->\n            {ok,Value}\n    catch\n        error:{badkey,Key} ->\n            undefined\n    end;\nretrieve(ConnectionHandler, Key) ->\n    call(ConnectionHandler, {retrieve,Key}).\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n%% . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .\nset_sock_opts(ConnectionRef, SocketOptions) ->\n    try lists:foldr(fun({Name,_Val}, Acc) ->\n                            case prohibited_sock_option(Name) of\n                                true -> [Name|Acc];\n                                false -> Acc\n                            end\n                    end, [], SocketOptions)\n    of\n        [] ->\n            call(ConnectionRef, {set_sock_opts,SocketOptions});\n        Bad ->\n            {error, {not_allowed,Bad}}\n    catch\n        _:_ ->\n            {error, badarg}\n    end.\n\nprohibited_sock_option(active)    -> true;\nprohibited_sock_option(deliver)   -> true;\nprohibited_sock_option(mode)      -> true;\nprohibited_sock_option(packet)    -> true;\nprohibited_sock_option(_) -> false.\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n%% . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .\nget_sock_opts(ConnectionRef, SocketGetOptions) ->\n    call(ConnectionRef, {get_sock_opts,SocketGetOptions}).\n\n%%====================================================================\n%% Test support\n%%====================================================================\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n-spec renegotiate(connection_ref()\n\t\t ) -> ok.\n%% . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .\nrenegotiate(ConnectionHandler) ->\n    cast(ConnectionHandler, force_renegotiate).\n\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\nalg(ConnectionHandler) ->\n    call(ConnectionHandler, get_alg).\n\n%%====================================================================\n%% Intitialisation\n%%====================================================================\ninit([Role, Socket, Opts]) when Role==client ; Role==server ->\n    process_flag(trap_exit, true),\n    %% ssh_params will be updated after receiving socket_control event\n    %% in wait_for_socket state;\n    D = #data{socket = Socket, ssh_params = #ssh{role = Role, opts = Opts}},\n    {ok, {wait_for_socket, Role}, D}.\n\n%%%----------------------------------------------------------------\n%%% Connection start and initialization helpers\ninit_connection_record(Role, Socket, Opts) ->\n    {WinSz, PktSz} = init_inet_buffers_window(Socket),\n    C = #connection{channel_cache = ssh_client_channel:cache_create(),\n                    channel_id_seed = 0,\n                    suggest_window_size = WinSz,\n                    suggest_packet_size = PktSz,\n                    requests = [],\n                    options = Opts,\n                    connection_supervisor = ?GET_INTERNAL_OPT(connection_sup, Opts)\n                   },\n    case Role of\n        server ->\n            C#connection{cli_spec =\n                             ?GET_OPT(ssh_cli, Opts, {ssh_cli,[?GET_OPT(shell, Opts)]}),\n                         exec =\n                             ?GET_OPT(exec, Opts)};\n        client ->\n            C\n    end.\n\ninit_ssh_record(Role, Socket, Opts) ->\n    %% Export of this internal function is\n    %% intended for low-level protocol test suites\n    {ok,PeerAddr} = inet:peername(Socket),\n    init_ssh_record(Role, Socket, PeerAddr, Opts).\n\ninit_ssh_record(Role, Socket, PeerAddr, Opts) ->\n    AuthMethods = ?GET_OPT(auth_methods, Opts),\n    S0 = #ssh{role = Role,\n\t      opts = Opts,\n\t      userauth_supported_methods = AuthMethods,\n\t      available_host_keys = available_hkey_algorithms(Role, Opts),\n\t      random_length_padding = ?GET_OPT(max_random_length_padding, Opts)\n\t   },\n\n    {Vsn, Version} = ssh_transport:versions(Role, Opts),\n    LocalName = case inet:sockname(Socket) of\n                    {ok,Local} -> Local;\n                    _ -> undefined\n                end,\n    case Role of\n\tclient ->\n\t    PeerName = case ?GET_INTERNAL_OPT(host, Opts, element(1,PeerAddr)) of\n                           PeerIP when is_tuple(PeerIP) ->\n                               inet_parse:ntoa(PeerIP);\n                           PeerName0 when is_atom(PeerName0) ->\n                               atom_to_list(PeerName0);\n                           PeerName0 when is_list(PeerName0) ->\n                               PeerName0\n                       end,\n            ssh_lib:set_label(client,\n                              {connection, list_to_binary(PeerName), Socket}),\n            S1 =\n                S0#ssh{c_vsn = Vsn,\n                       c_version = Version,\n                       opts = ?PUT_INTERNAL_OPT({io_cb, case ?GET_OPT(user_interaction, Opts) of\n                                                            true ->  ssh_io;\n                                                            false -> ssh_no_io\n                                                        end},\n                                                Opts),\n                       userauth_quiet_mode = ?GET_OPT(quiet_mode, Opts),\n                       peer = {PeerName, PeerAddr},\n                       local = LocalName\n                      },\n            S1#ssh{userauth_pubkeys = [K || K <- ?GET_OPT(pref_public_key_algs, Opts),\n                                            is_usable_user_pubkey(K, S1)\n                                      ]\n                  };\n\n\tserver ->\n            ssh_lib:set_label(server, {connection, Socket}),\n\t    S0#ssh{s_vsn = Vsn,\n\t\t   s_version = Version,\n\t\t   userauth_methods = string:tokens(AuthMethods, \",\"),\n\t\t   kb_tries_left = 3,\n\t\t   peer = {undefined, PeerAddr},\n                   local = LocalName\n\t\t  }\n    end.\n\nhandshake(ConnPid, server, Ref, Timeout) ->\n    receive\n\t{ConnPid, ssh_connected} ->\n\t    erlang:demonitor(Ref, [flush]),\n\t    {ok, ConnPid};\n\t{ConnPid, {not_connected, Reason}} ->\n\t    erlang:demonitor(Ref, [flush]),\n\t    {error, Reason};\n\t{'DOWN', Ref, process, ConnPid, {shutdown, Reason}} ->\n\t    {error, Reason};\n\t{'DOWN', Ref, process, ConnPid, Reason} ->\n\t    {error, Reason};\n        {'EXIT',_,Reason} ->\n            stop(ConnPid),\n            {error, {exit,Reason}}\n    after Timeout ->\n\t    erlang:demonitor(Ref, [flush]),\n\t    ssh_connection_handler:stop(ConnPid),\n\t    {error, timeout}\n    end;\nhandshake(ConnPid, client, Ref, Timeout) ->\n    receive\n\t{ConnPid, ssh_connected} ->\n\t    erlang:demonitor(Ref, [flush]),\n\t    {ok, ConnPid};\n\t{ConnPid, {not_connected, Reason}} ->\n\t    erlang:demonitor(Ref, [flush]),\n\t    {error, Reason};\n\t{'DOWN', Ref, process, ConnPid, {shutdown, Reason}} ->\n\t    {error, Reason};\n\t{'DOWN', Ref, process, ConnPid, Reason} ->\n\t    {error, Reason}\n    after Timeout ->\n\t    erlang:demonitor(Ref, [flush]),\n\t    ssh_connection_handler:stop(ConnPid),\n\t    {error, timeout}\n    end.\n\nhandshake(Msg, #data{starter = User}) ->\n    User ! {self(), Msg}.\n\n%%====================================================================\n%% gen_statem callbacks\n%%====================================================================\n%%--------------------------------------------------------------------\n-type event_content() ::  any().\n\n-type renegotiate_flag() :: init | renegotiate.\n\n-type state_name() :: \n        {hello,                     role()                    }\n      | {kexinit,                   role(), renegotiate_flag()}\n      | {key_exchange,              role(), renegotiate_flag()}\n      | {key_exchange_dh_gex_init,  server, renegotiate_flag()}\n      | {key_exchange_dh_gex_reply, client, renegotiate_flag()}\n      | {new_keys,                  role(), renegotiate_flag()}\n      | {ext_info,                  role(), renegotiate_flag()}\n      | {service_request,           role()                    }\n      | {userauth,                  role()                    }\n      | {userauth_keyboard_interactive,       role()          }\n      | {userauth_keyboard_interactive_extra, server          }\n      | {userauth_keyboard_interactive_info_response, client  }\n      | {connected,                 role()                    }\n\t.\n\n%% The state names must fulfill some rules regarding\n%% where the role() and the renegotiate_flag() is placed:\n\n-spec renegotiation(state_name()) -> boolean().\nrenegotiation({_,_,ReNeg}) -> ReNeg == renegotiate;\nrenegotiation(_) -> false.\n\n\n-define(CONNECTED(StateName),\n        (element(1,StateName) == connected orelse\n         element(1,StateName) == ext_info ) ).\n\n-spec handle_event(gen_statem:event_type(),\n\t\t   event_content(),\n\t\t   state_name(),\n\t\t   #data{}\n\t\t  ) -> gen_statem:event_handler_result(state_name()) .\n\n-define(CONNECTION_MSG(Msg),\n        [{next_event, internal, prepare_next_packet},\n         {next_event,internal,{conn_msg,Msg}}]).\n\n%% . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .\ncallback_mode() ->\n    [handle_event_function,\n     state_enter].\n\n%%% ######## {hello, client|server} ####\n%% The very first event that is sent when ssh_connection_handler\n%% becomes owner process for Socket\nhandle_event(cast, socket_control, {wait_for_socket, Role},\n             #data{socket = Socket, ssh_params = #ssh{opts = Opts}}) ->\n    case inet:peername(Socket) of\n        {ok, PeerAddr} ->\n            try\n                {Protocol, Callback, CloseTag} = ?GET_OPT(transport, Opts),\n                D = #data{starter = ?GET_INTERNAL_OPT(user_pid, Opts),\n                          socket = Socket,\n                          transport_protocol = Protocol,\n                          transport_cb = Callback,\n                          transport_close_tag = CloseTag,\n                          ssh_params = init_ssh_record(Role, Socket, PeerAddr, Opts),\n                          connection_state = init_connection_record(Role, Socket, Opts)\n                         },\n                NextEvent = {next_event, internal, socket_ready},\n                {next_state, {hello,Role}, D, NextEvent}\n            catch\n                _:{error,Error} -> {stop, {error,Error}};\n                error:Error ->     {stop, {error,Error}}\n            end;\n\n        {error,Error} ->\n            {stop, {shutdown,Error}}\n    end;\n\nhandle_event(internal, socket_ready, {hello,_}=StateName, #data{ssh_params = Ssh0} = D) ->\n    VsnMsg = ssh_transport:hello_version_msg(string_version(Ssh0)),\n    send_bytes(VsnMsg, D),\n    case inet:getopts(Socket=D#data.socket, [recbuf]) of\n\t{ok, [{recbuf,Size}]} ->\n\t    %% Set the socket to the hello text line handling mode:\n\t    inet:setopts(Socket, [{packet, line},\n\t\t\t\t  {active, once},\n\t\t\t\t  % Expecting the version string which might\n\t\t\t\t  % be max ?MAX_PROTO_VERSION bytes:\n\t\t\t\t  {recbuf, ?MAX_PROTO_VERSION},\n\t\t\t\t  {nodelay,true}]),\n            Time = ?GET_OPT(hello_timeout, Ssh0#ssh.opts, infinity),\n\t    {keep_state, D#data{inet_initial_recbuf_size=Size}, [{state_timeout,Time,no_hello_received}] };\n\n\tOther ->\n            ?call_disconnectfun_and_log_cond(\"Option return\", \n                                             io_lib:format(\"Unexpected getopts return:~n  ~p\",[Other]),\n                                             StateName, D),\n\t    {stop, {shutdown,{unexpected_getopts_return, Other}}}\n    end;\n\nhandle_event(internal, {info_line,_Line}, {hello,client}, D) ->\n    %% The server may send info lines to the client before the version_exchange\n    %% RFC4253/4.2\n    inet:setopts(D#data.socket, [{active, once}]),\n    keep_state_and_data;\n\nhandle_event(internal, {info_line,Line}, {hello,server}=StateName, D) ->\n    %% But the client may NOT send them to the server. Openssh answers with cleartext,\n    %% and so do we\n    send_bytes(\"Protocol mismatch.\", D),\n    Msg = io_lib:format(\"Protocol mismatch in version exchange. Client sent info lines.~n~s\",\n                        [ssh_dbg:hex_dump(Line, 64)]),\n    ?call_disconnectfun_and_log_cond(\"Protocol mismatch.\", Msg, StateName, D),\n    {stop, {shutdown,\"Protocol mismatch in version exchange. Client sent info lines.\"}};\n\nhandle_event(internal, {version_exchange,Version}, {hello,Role}, D0) ->\n    {NumVsn, StrVsn} = ssh_transport:handle_hello_version(Version),\n    case handle_version(NumVsn, StrVsn, D0#data.ssh_params) of\n\t{ok, Ssh1} ->\n\t    %% Since the hello part is finished correctly, we set the\n\t    %% socket to the packet handling mode (including recbuf size):\n\t    inet:setopts(D0#data.socket, [{packet,0},\n\t\t\t\t\t {mode,binary},\n\t\t\t\t\t {active, once},\n\t\t\t\t\t {recbuf, D0#data.inet_initial_recbuf_size}]),\n\t    {KeyInitMsg, SshPacket, Ssh} = ssh_transport:key_exchange_init_msg(Ssh1),\n\t    send_bytes(SshPacket, D0),\n            D = D0#data{ssh_params = Ssh,\n                        key_exchange_init_msg = KeyInitMsg},\n\t    {next_state, {kexinit,Role,init}, D, {change_callback_module, ssh_fsm_kexinit}};\n\n\tnot_supported ->\n            {Shutdown, D} =\n                ?send_disconnect(?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_VERSION_NOT_SUPPORTED,\n                                 io_lib:format(\"Offending version is ~p\",[string:chomp(Version)]),\n                                 {hello,Role},\n                                 D0),\n\t    {stop, Shutdown, D}\n    end;\n\n%%% timeout after tcp:connect but then nothing arrives\nhandle_event(state_timeout, no_hello_received, {hello,_Role}=StateName, D0 = #data{ssh_params = Ssh0}) ->\n    MsgFun =\n        fun (debug) ->\n                Time = ?GET_OPT(hello_timeout, Ssh0#ssh.opts),\n                lists:concat([\"No HELLO received within \",ssh_lib:format_time_ms(Time)]);\n            (_) ->\n                [\"No HELLO received within hello_timeout\"]\n        end,\n    {Shutdown, D} =\n        ?send_disconnect(?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR, ?SELECT_MSG(MsgFun), StateName, D0),\n    {stop, Shutdown, D};\n\n\n%%% ######## {service_request, client|server} ####\n\nhandle_event(internal, Msg = #ssh_msg_service_request{name=ServiceName}, StateName = {service_request,server}, D0) ->\n    case ServiceName of\n\t\"ssh-userauth\" ->\n\t    Ssh0 = #ssh{session_id=SessionId} = D0#data.ssh_params,\n\t    {ok, {Reply, Ssh}} = ssh_auth:handle_userauth_request(Msg, SessionId, Ssh0),\n            D = send_msg(Reply, D0#data{ssh_params = Ssh}),\n\t    {next_state, {userauth,server}, D, {change_callback_module,ssh_fsm_userauth_server}};\n\n\t_ ->\n            {Shutdown, D} =\n                ?send_disconnect(?SSH_DISCONNECT_SERVICE_NOT_AVAILABLE,\n                                 io_lib:format(\"Unknown service: ~p\",[ServiceName]),\n                                 StateName, D0),\n            {stop, Shutdown, D}\n    end;\n\nhandle_event(internal, #ssh_msg_service_accept{name = \"ssh-userauth\"}, {service_request,client},\n\t     #data{ssh_params = #ssh{service=\"ssh-userauth\"} = Ssh0} = D0) ->\n    {Msg, Ssh} = ssh_auth:init_userauth_request_msg(Ssh0),\n    D = send_msg(Msg, D0#data{ssh_params = Ssh,\n                              auth_user = Ssh#ssh.user\n                             }),\n    {next_state, {userauth,client}, D, {change_callback_module,ssh_fsm_userauth_client}};\n\n\n%% Skip ext_info messages in connected state (for example from OpenSSH >= 7.7)\nhandle_event(internal, #ssh_msg_ext_info{}, {connected,_Role}, D) ->\n    {keep_state, D};\n\nhandle_event(internal, {#ssh_msg_kexinit{},_}, {connected,Role}, D0) ->\n    {KeyInitMsg, SshPacket, Ssh} = ssh_transport:key_exchange_init_msg(D0#data.ssh_params),\n    D = D0#data{ssh_params = Ssh,\n\t\tkey_exchange_init_msg = KeyInitMsg},\n    send_bytes(SshPacket, D),\n    {next_state, {kexinit,Role,renegotiate}, D, [postpone, {change_callback_module,ssh_fsm_kexinit}]};\n\nhandle_event(internal, #ssh_msg_disconnect{description=Desc} = Msg, StateName, D0) ->\n    {disconnect, _, RepliesCon} =\n\tssh_connection:handle_msg(Msg, D0#data.connection_state, ?role(StateName), D0#data.ssh_params),\n    {Actions,D} = send_replies(RepliesCon, D0),\n    disconnect_fun(\"Received disconnect: \"++Desc, D),\n    {stop_and_reply, {shutdown,Desc}, Actions, D};\n\nhandle_event(internal, #ssh_msg_ignore{}, {_StateName, _Role, init},\n             #data{ssh_params = #ssh{kex_strict_negotiated = true,\n                                     send_sequence = SendSeq,\n                                     recv_sequence = RecvSeq}}) ->\n    ?DISCONNECT(?SSH_DISCONNECT_KEY_EXCHANGE_FAILED,\n                io_lib:format(\"strict KEX violation: unexpected SSH_MSG_IGNORE \"\n                              \"send_sequence = ~p  recv_sequence = ~p\",\n                              [SendSeq, RecvSeq])\n               );\n\nhandle_event(internal, #ssh_msg_ignore{}, _StateName, _) ->\n    keep_state_and_data;\n\nhandle_event(internal, #ssh_msg_unimplemented{}, _StateName, _) ->\n    keep_state_and_data;\n\n%% Quick fix of failing test case (ssh_options_SUITE:ssh_msg_debug_fun_option_{client|server}/1)\nhandle_event(cast, #ssh_msg_debug{} = Msg, State, D) ->\n    handle_event(internal, Msg, State, D);\n\nhandle_event(internal, #ssh_msg_debug{} = Msg, _StateName, D) ->\n    debug_fun(Msg, D),\n    keep_state_and_data;\n\nhandle_event(internal, {conn_msg,Msg}, StateName, #data{connection_state = Connection0,\n                                                        event_queue = Qev0} = D0) ->\n    Role = ?role(StateName),\n    Rengotation = renegotiation(StateName),\n    try ssh_connection:handle_msg(Msg, Connection0, Role, D0#data.ssh_params) of\n\t{disconnect, Reason0, RepliesConn} ->\n            {Repls, D} = send_replies(RepliesConn, D0),\n            case {Reason0,Role} of\n                {{_, Reason}, client} when ((StateName =/= {connected,client})\n                                            and (not Rengotation)) ->\n                    handshake({not_connected,Reason}, D);\n                _ ->\n                    ok\n            end,\n            {stop_and_reply, {shutdown,normal}, Repls, D};\n\n\t{Replies, Connection} when is_list(Replies) ->\n\t    {Repls, D} = \n\t\tcase StateName of\n\t\t    {connected,_} ->\n\t\t\tsend_replies(Replies, D0#data{connection_state=Connection});\n\t\t    _ ->\n\t\t\t{ConnReplies, NonConnReplies} = lists:splitwith(fun not_connected_filter/1, Replies),\n\t\t\tsend_replies(NonConnReplies, D0#data{event_queue = Qev0 ++ ConnReplies})\n\t\tend,\n            case {Msg, StateName} of\n                {#ssh_msg_channel_close{}, {connected,_}} ->\n                    {keep_state, D, [cond_set_idle_timer(D)|Repls]};\n                {#ssh_msg_channel_success{}, _} ->\n                    update_inet_buffers(D#data.socket),\n                    {keep_state, D, Repls};\n                _ ->\n                    {keep_state, D, Repls}\n            end\n\n    catch\n\tClass:Error ->\n            {Repls, D1} = send_replies(ssh_connection:handle_stop(Connection0), D0),\n            {Shutdown, D} = ?send_disconnect(?SSH_DISCONNECT_BY_APPLICATION,\n                                             io_lib:format(\"Internal error: ~p:~p\",[Class,Error]),\n                                             StateName, D1),\n            {stop_and_reply, Shutdown, Repls, D}\n    end;\n\n\nhandle_event(enter, OldState, {connected,_}=NewState, D) ->\n    %% Entering the state where re-negotiation is possible\n    init_renegotiate_timers(OldState, NewState, D);\n\nhandle_event(enter, OldState, {ext_info,_,renegotiate}=NewState, D) ->\n    %% Could be hanging in exit_info state if nothing else arrives\n    init_renegotiate_timers(OldState, NewState, D);\n\nhandle_event(enter, {connected,_}=OldState, NewState, D) ->\n    %% Exiting the state where re-negotiation is possible\n    pause_renegotiate_timers(OldState, NewState, D);\n\nhandle_event(cast, force_renegotiate, StateName, D) ->\n    handle_event({timeout,renegotiate}, undefined, StateName, D);\n\nhandle_event({timeout,renegotiate}, _, StateName, D0) ->\n    case StateName of\n        {connected,Role} ->\n            start_rekeying(Role, D0);\n        {ext_info,Role,renegotiate} ->\n            start_rekeying(Role, D0);\n        _ ->\n            %% Wrong state for starting a renegotiation, must be in re-negotiation\n            keep_state_and_data\n    end;\n\nhandle_event({timeout,check_data_size}, _, StateName, D0) ->\n    %% Rekey due to sent data limit reached? (Can't be in {ext_info,...} if data is sent)\n    case StateName of\n        {connected,Role} ->\n            check_data_rekeying(Role, D0);\n        _ ->\n            %% Wrong state for starting a renegotiation, must be in re-negotiation\n            keep_state_and_data\n    end;\n\nhandle_event({call,From}, get_alg, _, D) ->\n    #ssh{algorithms=Algs} = D#data.ssh_params,\n    {keep_state_and_data, [{reply,From,Algs}]};\n\nhandle_event(cast, _, StateName, _) when not ?CONNECTED(StateName) ->\n    {keep_state_and_data, [postpone]};\n\nhandle_event(cast, {adjust_window,ChannelId,Bytes}, StateName, D) when ?CONNECTED(StateName) ->\n    case ssh_client_channel:cache_lookup(cache(D), ChannelId) of\n\t#channel{recv_window_size = WinSize,\n\t\t recv_window_pending = Pending,\n\t\t recv_packet_size = PktSize} = Channel\n\t  when (WinSize-Bytes) >= 2*PktSize ->\n\t    %% The peer can send at least two more *full* packet, no hurry.\n\t    ssh_client_channel:cache_update(cache(D),\n\t\t\t\t     Channel#channel{recv_window_pending = Pending + Bytes}),\n\t    keep_state_and_data;\n\n\t#channel{recv_window_size = WinSize,\n\t\t recv_window_pending = Pending,\n\t\t remote_id = Id} = Channel ->\n\t    %% Now we have to update the window - we can't receive so many more pkts\n\t    ssh_client_channel:cache_update(cache(D),\n\t\t\t\t     Channel#channel{recv_window_size =\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t WinSize + Bytes + Pending,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t     recv_window_pending = 0}),\n\t    Msg = {reply_request,Resp,ChannelId}, StateName, D) when ?CONNECTED(StateName) ->\n    case ssh_client_channel:cache_lookup(cache(D), ChannelId) of\n        #channel{remote_id = RemoteId} when Resp== ssh_connection:channel_failure_msg(RemoteId)\n                end,\n            update_inet_buffers(D#data.socket),\n            {keep_state, send_msg(Msg,D)};\n\n        #channel{} ->\n            Details = io_lib:format(\"Unhandled reply in state ~p:~n~p\", [StateName,Resp]),\n            {_Shutdown, D1} =\n                ?send_disconnect(?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR, Details, StateName, ->\n    {keep_state,  handle_request(ChannelPid, ChannelId, Type, Data, false, none, D)};\n\nhandle_event(cast, {request,ChannelId,Type,Data}, StateName, D) when ?CONNECTED(StateName) ->\n    {keep_state,  handle_request(ChannelId, Type, Data, false, none, D)};\n\nhandle_event(cast, {unknown,Data}, StateName, D) when ?CONNECTED(StateName) ->\n    Msg = #ssh_msg_unimplemented{sequence =
