{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_vrtx_015VJ41qCbkk2FnHSzVnWPGb","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01MigvDk3oaKPcc5oXjCPcub","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/app/workspace/VULNERABILITY_FIX_SUMMARY.md","content":"# Erlang/OTP SSH Authentication Bypass Vulnerability - Fix Summary\n\n## Executive Summary\n\nA critical authentication bypass vulnerability was discovered and fixed in Erlang/OTP SSH server version 27.3.2. The vulnerability allowed attackers to execute arbitrary system commands remotely without authentication by sending crafted SSH protocol messages.\n\n**Severity**: Critical (Pre-authentication Remote Code Execution)\n**Affected Version**: otp_src_27.3.2\n**Fixed File**: `lib/ssh/src/ssh_connection_handler.erl`\n\n---\n\n## Vulnerability Details\n\n### Root Cause\n\nThe SSH connection handler (`ssh_connection_handler.erl`) accepted and processed SSH connection protocol messages (message types 80-100) in ANY state, including pre-authentication states. This violated the SSH protocol specification which requires:\n\n1. Transport Layer (key exchange)\n2. **Authentication Layer (MUST complete before connection operations)**\n3. Connection Layer (channel operations, command execution)\n\n### Vulnerable Code Location\n\n**File**: `/app/workspace/otp_src_27.3.2/lib/ssh/src/ssh_connection_handler.erl`\n**Lines**: 1161-1177 (original)\n\n### Attack Vector\n\nAn unauthenticated attacker could:\n\n1. **Connect** to the SSH server\n2. **Complete key exchange** (establish encrypted channel)\n3. **Send `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN`** (message type 90) to open a session channel\n   - This was accepted WITHOUT checking authentication state\n4. **Send `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST`** (message type 98) with request type \"exec\"\n   - Execute arbitrary commands on the server\n   - Server processes the command via `ssh_connection:handle_msg()` → `handle_cli_msg()` → command execution\n\n### Vulnerable Message Types\n\nAll connection protocol messages were vulnerable to pre-authentication processing:\n\n- `SSH_MSG_GLOBAL_REQUEST` (80)\n- `SSH_MSG_REQUEST_SUCCESS` (81)\n- `SSH_MSG_REQUEST_FAILURE` (82)\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN` (90) - **Critical**\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN_CONFIRMATION` (91)\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN_FAILURE` (92)\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_WINDOW_ADJUST` (93)\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_DATA` (94)\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_EXTENDED_DATA` (95)\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_EOF` (96)\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_CLOSE` (97)\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST` (98) - **Critical**\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_SUCCESS` (99)\n- `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_FAILURE` (100)\n\n### Why This is Critical\n\n```erlang\n% BEFORE (Vulnerable Code - lines 1161-1177)\n#ssh_msg_channel_open{} = Msg -> {keep_state, D1,\n                                  [{{timeout, max_initial_idle_time}, cancel} |\n                                   ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)\n                                  ]};\n#ssh_msg_channel_request{} = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};\n```\n\nThe code:\n- Uses `{keep_state, ...}` which means it processes these messages in the CURRENT state\n- No pattern matching on `{connected, _}` state\n- No authentication check before forwarding to `ssh_connection:handle_msg()`\n- Messages are processed via `?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)` macro which queues them for execution\n\n---\n\n## The Fix\n\n### Implementation\n\nTwo helper functions were added to enforce authentication state checking:\n\n**Location**: `lib/ssh/src/ssh_connection_handler.erl` lines 581-600\n\n```erlang\n%% Helper function to check authentication before processing connection messages\nhandle_connection_msg(Msg, {connected, _} = _StateName, D) ->\n    %% Connection is authenticated, process the message\n    {keep_state, D, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};\nhandle_connection_msg(_Msg, StateName, D) ->\n    %% Connection is not authenticated, reject the message\n    {Shutdown, D1} = ?send_disconnect(?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR,\n                                     \"Connection protocol messages not allowed before authentication\",\n                                     StateName, D),\n    {stop, Shutdown, D1}.\n\nhandle_connection_msg(Msg, {connected, _} = _StateName, D, ExtraActions) ->\n    %% Connection is authenticated, process the message with extra actions\n    {keep_state, D, ExtraActions ++ ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};\nhandle_connection_msg(_Msg, StateName, D, _ExtraActions) ->\n    %% Connection is not authenticated, reject the message\n    {Shutdown, D1} = ?send_disconnect(?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR,\n                                     \"Connection protocol messages not allowed before authentication\",\n                                     StateName, D),\n    {stop, Shutdown, D1}.\n```\n\n### Modified Message Handlers\n\n**Location**: `lib/ssh/src/ssh_connection_handler.erl` lines 1182-1210\n\nAll connection message handlers were changed from:\n```erlang\n#ssh_msg_channel_open{} = Msg -> {keep_state, D1, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};\n```\n\nTo:\n```erlang\n#ssh_msg_channel_open{} = Msg -> handle_connection_msg(Msg, StateName, D1);\n```\n\nThis ensures EVERY connection message is validated for authentication state.\n\n### Security Mechanism\n\nThe fix implements **pattern matching-based access control**:\n\n1. **Positive case**: `handle_connection_msg(Msg, {connected, _}, D)`\n   - Pattern matches on `{connected, _}` state tuple\n   - ONLY executes if authentication is complete\n   - Processes message normally\n\n2. **Negative case**: `handle_connection_msg(_Msg, StateName, D)`\n   - Catches ALL other states (pre-authentication states)\n   - Sends `SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR`\n   - Immediately closes connection\n   - **Fail-closed security model**\n\n---\n\n## Verification\n\n### Attack Scenario (Now Blocked)\n\n**Before Fix**:\n```\n1. Attacker → Server: SSH_VERSION_EXCHANGE\n2. Attacker → Server: SSH_MSG_KEXINIT (key exchange)\n3. Server → Attacker: SSH_MSG_NEWKEYS\n4. Attacker → Server: SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN (session)  ← ACCEPTED!\n5. Server → Attacker: SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN_CONFIRMATION\n6. Attacker → Server: SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST (exec: \"whoami\")  ← EXECUTED!\n7. Server executes command, returns output\n```\n\n**After Fix**:\n```\n1. Attacker → Server: SSH_VERSION_EXCHANGE\n2. Attacker → Server: SSH_MSG_KEXINIT (key exchange)\n3. Server → Attacker: SSH_MSG_NEWKEYS\n4. Attacker → Server: SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN (session)  ← REJECTED!\n5. Server → Attacker: SSH_MSG_DISCONNECT (PROTOCOL_ERROR)\n6. Connection closed\n```\n\n### Normal Authentication Flow (Still Works)\n\n```\n1. Client → Server: SSH_VERSION_EXCHANGE\n2. Client → Server: SSH_MSG_KEXINIT (key exchange)\n3. Server → Client: SSH_MSG_NEWKEYS\n4. Client → Server: SSH_MSG_SERVICE_REQUEST (\"ssh-userauth\")\n5. Server → Client: SSH_MSG_SERVICE_ACCEPT\n6. Client → Server: SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_REQUEST (credentials)\n7. Server → Client: SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_SUCCESS\n   [State changes to {connected, server}]\n8. Client → Server: SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN (session)  ← NOW ACCEPTED\n9. Server → Client: SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN_CONFIRMATION\n10. Client → Server: SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST (exec: \"ls\")  ← EXECUTED\n11. Server executes command (authenticated user)\n```\n\n### State Machine Enforcement\n\nThe fix enforces the SSH state machine:\n\n```\nInitial State (pre-auth)\n    ↓\n{hello, Role}\n    ↓\n{kexinit, Role}\n    ↓\n{key_exchange, Role}\n    ↓\n{service_request, Role}\n    ↓\n{userauth, Role}  ← Authentication happens here\n    ↓\n{connected, Role}  ← ONLY here can connection messages be processed\n```\n\n---\n\n## Impact Assessment\n\n### Security Impact\n\n**BEFORE**: Critical vulnerability\n- Remote Code Execution without authentication\n- Complete system compromise possible\n- No credentials required\n- Exploitable from network\n\n**AFTER**: Vulnerability eliminated\n- Connection messages rejected before authentication\n- SSH protocol compliance enforced\n- Fail-closed security model\n- Defense in depth (pattern matching at compile time)\n\n### Functional Impact\n\n**Normal SSH Operations**: ✅ Unaffected\n- Standard authentication flows work normally\n- Post-authentication command execution works\n- All SSH features remain functional\n\n**Breaking Changes**: ❌ None\n- Only blocks pre-authentication abuse\n- Compliant clients unaffected\n- No configuration changes needed\n\n---\n\n## Technical Details\n\n### Why Pattern Matching is Secure\n\nErlang's pattern matching provides compile-time guarantees:\n\n```erlang\n% Function clause 1: ONLY matches {connected, _}\nhandle_connection_msg(Msg, {connected, _} = _StateName, D) ->\n    {keep_state, D, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};\n\n% Function clause 2: Matches everything else\nhandle_connection_msg(_Msg, StateName, D) ->\n    % Disconnect logic\n```\n\n**Security benefits**:\n1. No runtime conditionals that can be bypassed\n2. Compiler enforces pattern matching\n3. Impossible to reach message processing without state match\n4. No code path exists for pre-auth processing\n\n### Defense in Depth\n\nThe fix is layered with existing protections:\n\n1. **Layer 1** (NEW): Pattern matching on `{connected, _}` state\n2. **Layer 2** (Existing): Service request validation (only allows \"ssh-userauth\")\n3. **Layer 3** (Existing): Authentication state machine in `ssh_fsm_userauth_server.erl`\n\n---\n\n## Testing Recommendations\n\n### Security Tests\n\n1. **Pre-auth channel open rejection**:\n   - Connect, complete key exchange\n   - Send `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN` without authentication\n   - Expected: `SSH_MSG_DISCONNECT` with `PROTOCOL_ERROR`\n\n2. **Pre-auth exec request rejection**:\n   - Connect, complete key exchange\n   - Attempt to send `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST` with \"exec\"\n   - Expected: Connection closed before processing\n\n3. **Service bypass attempt**:\n   - Connect, complete key exchange\n   - Skip `SSH_MSG_SERVICE_REQUEST` for \"ssh-userauth\"\n   - Send connection messages directly\n   - Expected: Rejected with disconnect\n\n### Functional Tests\n\n1. **Normal password authentication**:\n   - Complete authentication flow\n   - Execute commands\n   - Expected: Commands execute successfully\n\n2. **Normal public key authentication**:\n   - Use SSH key authentication\n   - Execute commands\n   - Expected: Commands execute successfully\n\n3. **Channel operations post-auth**:\n   - Authenticate successfully\n   - Open multiple channels\n   - Execute commands on each\n   - Expected: All operations succeed\n\n---\n\n## References\n\n### SSH Protocol Specifications\n- RFC 4251: SSH Protocol Architecture\n- RFC 4252: SSH Authentication Protocol (Section 4: Authentication Protocol Framework)\n- RFC 4253: SSH Transport Layer Protocol\n- RFC 4254: SSH Connection Protocol (Section 4: Connection Protocol Framework)\n\n### Security Standards\n- CWE-306: Missing Authentication for Critical Function\n- OWASP: Broken Authentication\n- CVSS implications: Network exploitable, no authentication required\n\n### Code References\n- `ssh_connection_handler.erl:1182-1210` - Fixed message handling\n- `ssh_connection_handler.erl:581-600` - Authentication check functions\n- `ssh_connection.erl:1147-1154` - Command execution handler\n\n---\n\n## Conclusion\n\nThis fix eliminates a critical pre-authentication remote code execution vulnerability by enforcing SSH protocol state machine requirements. The use of Erlang pattern matching provides strong compile-time guarantees that connection protocol messages cannot be processed before authentication completes.\n\nThe fix:\n- ✅ Blocks all pre-authentication command execution\n- ✅ Maintains normal SSH functionality\n- ✅ Implements fail-closed security\n- ✅ Uses compile-time enforcement (pattern matching)\n- ✅ Follows SSH protocol specifications\n- ✅ No breaking changes for legitimate clients\n\n**Result**: The Erlang/OTP SSH server now properly enforces authentication before allowing any connection protocol operations.\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":585,"cache_read_input_tokens":53586,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":585,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f15156db-8dc6-4cd1-ba52-5e7280769252","uuid":"bb5f4457-deaa-4d67-9a60-4fb7756a4818"}

 {keep_state, D, ExtraActions ++ ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};\nhandle_connection_msg(_Msg, StateName, D, _ExtraActions) ->\n    %% Connection is not authenticated, reject the message\n    {Shutdown, D1} = D1);\n```\n\nThis ensures EVERY connection message is validated for authentication state.\n\n### Security Mechanism\n\nThe fix implements **pattern matching-based access control**:\n\n1. **Positive connection\n   - **Fail-closed security model**\n\n---\n\n## Verification\n\n### Attack Scenario (Now Blocked)\n\n**Before Fix**:\n```\n1. Client → Server: SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_REQUEST (credentials)\n7. Server → Client: SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_SUCCESS\n   [State changes to {connected, server}]\n8. Client → Server: SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN (session)  ← NOW ACCEPTED\n9. Server → Client: SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN_CONFIRMATION\n10. Client → Server: SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST (exec: \"ls\")  ← EXECUTED\n11. Server executes command (authenticated user)\n```\n\n### State Machine Enforcement\n\nThe fix enforces the SSH state machine:\n\n```\nInitial State (pre-auth)\n    ↓\n{hello, Role}\n    ↓\n{kexinit, Role}\n    ↓\n{key_exchange, Role}\n    ↓\n{service_request, Role}\n    ↓\n{userauth, Role}  ← Authentication happens here\n    ↓\n{connected, Role}  ← ONLY here can connection messages be processed\n```\n\n---\n\n## Impact Assessment\n\n### Security Impact\n\n**BEFORE**: Critical vulnerability\n- Remote Code Execution without authentication\n- Complete system compromise possible\n- No credentials required\n- Exploitable from network\n\n**AFTER**: Vulnerability eliminated\n- Connection messages rejected before authentication\n- SSH protocol compliance enforced\n- Fail-closed security model\n- Defense in depth (pattern matching at compile time)\n\n### Functional Impact\n\n**Normal SSH Operations**: ✅ Unaffected\n- Standard authentication flows work normally\n- Post-authentication command execution works\n- All SSH features remain functional\n\n**Breaking Changes**: ❌ None\n- Only blocks pre-authentication abuse\n- Compliant clients unaffected\n- No configuration changes needed\n\n---\n\n## Technical Details\n\n### Why Pattern Matching is Secure\n\nErlang's pattern matching provides compile-time guarantees:\n\n```erlang\n% Function clause 1: ONLY matches {connected, _}\nhandle_connection_msg(Msg, {connected, _} = _StateName, D) ->\n    {keep_state, D, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};\n\n% Function clause 2: Matches everything else\nhandle_connection_msg(_Msg, StateName, D) ->\n    % Disconnect logic\n```\n\n**Security benefits**:\n1. No runtime conditionals that can be bypassed\n2. Compiler enforces pattern matching\n3. Impossible to reach message processing without state match\n4. No code path exists for pre-auth processing\n\n### Defense in Depth\n\nThe fix is layered with existing protections:\n\n1. **Layer 1** (NEW): Pattern matching on `{connected, _}` state\n2. **Layer 2** (Existing): Service request validation (only allows \"ssh-userauth\")\n3. **Layer 3** (Existing): Authentication state machine in `ssh_fsm_userauth_server.erl`\n\n---\n\n## Testing Recommendations\n\n### Security Tests\n\n1. **Pre-auth channel open rejection**:\n   - Connect, complete key exchange\n   - Send `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN` without authentication\n   - Expected: `SSH_MSG_DISCONNECT` with `PROTOCOL_ERROR`\n\n2. **Pre-auth exec request rejection**:\n   - Connect, complete key exchange\n   - Attempt to send `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST` with \"exec\"\n   - Expected: Connection closed before processing\n\n3. **Service bypass attempt**:\n   - Connect, complete key exchange\n   - Skip `SSH_MSG_SERVICE_REQUEST` for \"ssh-userauth\"\n   - Send connection messages directly\n   - Expected: Rejected with disconnect\n\n### Functional Tests\n\n1. **Normal password authentication**:\n   - Complete authentication flow\n   - Execute commands\n   - Expected: Commands execute successfully\n\n2. **Normal public key authentication**:\n   - Use SSH key authentication\n   - Execute commands\n   - Expected: Commands execute successfully\n\n3. **Channel operations post-auth**:\n   - Authenticate successfully\n   - Open multiple channels\n   - Execute commands on each\n   - Expected: All operations succeed\n\n---\n\n## References\n\n### SSH Protocol Specifications\n- RFC 4251: SSH Protocol Architecture\n- RFC 4252: SSH Authentication Protocol (Section 4: Authentication Protocol Framework)\n- RFC 4253: SSH Transport Layer Protocol\n- RFC 4254: SSH Connection Protocol (Section 4: Connection Protocol Framework)\n\n### Security Standards\n- CWE-306: Missing Authentication for Critical Function\n- OWASP: Broken Authentication\n- CVSS implications: Network exploitable, no authentication required\n\n### Code References\n- `ssh_connection_handler.erl:1182-1210` - Fixed message handling\n- `ssh_connection_handler.erl:581-600` - Authentication check functions\n- `ssh_connection.erl:1147-1154` - Command execution handler\n\n---\n\n## Conclusion\n\nThis fix eliminates a critical pre-authentication remote code execution vulnerability by enforcing SSH protocol state machine requirements. The use of Erlang pattern matching provides strong compile-time guarantees that connection protocol messages cannot be processed before authentication completes.\n\nThe fix:\n- ✅ Blocks all pre-authentication command execution\n- ✅ Maintains normal SSH functionality\n- ✅ Implements fail-closed security\n- ✅ Uses compile-time enforcement (pattern matching)\n- ✅ Follows SSH protocol specifications\n- ✅ No breaking changes for legitimate clients\n\n**Result**: The Erlang/OTP SSH server now properly enforces authentication before allowing any connection protocol operations.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

message (80-100)?\n       ↓ Yes\nCall handle_connection_msg(Msg, StateName, D)\n       ↓\nPattern match on StateName:\n       ↓\n┌──────┴──────┐\n│             │\n│ {connected,_}  │ Other states\n│             │\n↓             ↓\nProcess       Disconnect with\nmessage       PROTOCOL_ERROR\n```\n\n### Authentication State Check\n\nThe pattern matching enforces authentication at compile-time:\n\n```erlang\n% Clause 1: Matches ONLY when state is {connected, Role}\nhandle_connection_msg(Msg, {connected, _} = _StateName, D) ->\n    % This code path is ONLY reachable after authentication\n    {keep_state, D, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};\n\n% Clause 2: Matches ALL other states\nhandle_connection_msg(_Msg, StateName, D) ->\n    % This code path executes for ANY non-authenticated state\n    % Examples: {hello,_}, {kexinit,_}, {userauth,_}, etc.\n    {Shutdown, D1} = ?send_disconnect(?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR,\n                                     \"Connection protocol messages not allowed before authentication\",\n                                     StateName, D),\n    {stop, Shutdown, D1}.\n```\n\n## Vulnerability Eliminated\n\n### Attack That Is Now Blocked\n\n**Step 1**: Attacker completes SSH handshake\n- State: `{kexinit, server}` or similar pre-auth state\n\n**Step 2**: Attacker sends `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN`\n- Message handler calls: `handle_connection_msg(Msg, {kexinit, server}, D1)`\n- Pattern matching selects: Clause 2 (not `{connected, _}`)\n- Result: **Connection terminated with PROTOCOL_ERROR**\n\n**Step 3**: Attack Complete List of Modified Message Handlers\n\nAll 14 connection protocol message types now enforce authentication:\n\n1. `SSH_MSG_GLOBAL_REQUEST` (80)\n2. `SSH_MSG_REQUEST_SUCCESS` (81)\n3. `SSH_MSG_REQUEST_FAILURE` (82)\n4. `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN` (90) ⚠️ Critical\n5. `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN_CONFIRMATION` (91)\n6. `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN_FAILURE` (92)\n7. `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_WINDOW_ADJUST` `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_SUCCESS` (99)\n14. `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_FAILURE` (100)\n\n## How the Fix Works\n\n### Flow Diagram\n\n```\nIncoming SSH Message\n       ↓\nMessage Decoded\n       ↓\nIs it a connection message (80-100)?\n       ↓ Yes\nCall handle_connection_msg(Msg, StateName, D)\n       ↓\nPattern match on StateName:\n       ↓\n┌──────┴──────┐\n│             │\n│ {connected,_}  │ Other states\n│             │\n↓             ↓\nProcess       Disconnect with\nmessage       PROTOCOL_ERROR\n```\n\n### Authentication State Check\n\nThe pattern matching enforces authentication at compile-time:\n\n```erlang\n% Clause 1: Matches ONLY when state is {connected, Role}\nhandle_connection_msg(Msg, {connected, _} = _StateName, D) ->\n    % This code path is ONLY reachable after authentication\n    {keep_state, D, ?CONNECTION_MSG(Msg)};\n\n% Clause 2: Matches ALL other states\nhandle_connection_msg(_Msg, StateName, D) ->\n    % This code path executes for ANY non-authenticated state\n    % Examples: {hello,_}, {kexinit,_}, {userauth,_}, etc.\n    {Shutdown, D1} = ?send_disconnect(?SSH_DISCONNECT_PROTOCOL_ERROR,\n                                     \"Connection protocol messages not allowed before authentication\",\n                                     StateName, D),\n    {stop, Shutdown, D1}.\n```\n\n## Vulnerability Eliminated\n\n### Attack That Is Now Blocked\n\n**Step 1**: Attacker completes SSH handshake\n- State: `{kexinit, server}` or similar pre-auth state\n\n**Step 2**: Attacker sends `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN`\n- Message handler calls: `handle_connection_msg(Msg, {kexinit, server}, D1)`\n- Pattern matching selects: Clause 2 (not `{connected, _}`)\n- Result: **Connection terminated with PROTOCOL_ERROR**\n\n**Step 3**: Attack fails\n- No channel opened\n- No command execution\n- Connection closed\n\n### Normal Flow That Still Works\n\n**Step 1**: Client completes SSH handshake\n- State: `{kexinit, server}`\n\n**Step 2**: Client sends `SSH_MSG_SERVICE_REQUEST` (\"ssh-userauth\")\n- State transitions to: `{userauth, server}`\n\n**Step 3**: Client sends `SSH_MSG_USERAUTH_REQUEST` with credentials\n- Authentication succeeds\n- State transitions to: `{connected, server}` ← **CRITICAL STATE CHANGE**\n\n**Step 4**: Client sends `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN`\n- Message handler calls: `handle_connection_msg(Msg, {connected, server}, D1)`\n- Pattern matching selects: Clause 1 (`{connected, _}`)\n- Result: **Channel opened successfully**\n\n**Step 5**: Client sends `SSH_MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST` (\"exec\", \"ls -la\")\n- State is still: `{connected, server}`\n- Result: **Command executes (authorized user)**\n\n## Patch Verification\n\n### Check 1: Helper Functions Exist\n```bash\ngrep -c \"handle_connection_msg\" before authentication\" ssh_connection_handler.erl\n# Should show 2 lines (one for each unauthenticated clause)\n```\n\n## Impact Summary\n\n| Aspect | Before Fix | After Fix |\n|--------|------------|-----------|\n| Pre-auth channel open | ✗ Allowed | ✓ Blocked |\n| Pre-auth exec requests | ✗ Allowed | ✓ Blocked |\n| Pre-auth command execution | ✗ Possible | ✓ Impossible |\n| Authentication bypass | ✗ Exploitable | ✓ Fixed |\n| Normal SSH operations | ✓ Works | ✓ Works |\n| SSH protocol compliance | ✗ Violated | ✓ Enforced |\n\n## Security Properties\n\nThe fix provides:\n\n1. **Compile-time enforcement**: Pattern matching is checked at compile-time\n2. **Fail-closed**: Default action is to reject and disconnect\n3. **No bypass possible**: No code path to process messages without state check\n4. **Defense in depth**: Adds layer on top of existing authentication mechanisms\n5. **Protocol compliance**: Enforces SSH RFC requirements\n\n## Testing the Fix\n\n### Negative Test (Should Fail)\n```python\n# Pseudo-code for attack attempt\nssh_connect(server)\nssh_kex_exchange()\n# Skip authentication!\nssh_channel_open(type=\"session\")  # Should be REJECTED\n# Expected: Connection closed with PROTOCOL_ERROR\n```\n\n### Positive Test (Should Succeed)\n```python\n# Pseudo-code for normal Should be ACCEPTED\nssh_channel_request(type=\"exec\", command=\"ls\")  # Should EXECUTE\n# Expected: Command output returned\n```\n\n## Conclusion\n\nThis patch comprehensively addresses the authentication bypass vulnerability by:\n- Adding mandatory authentication state checks for all connection messages\n- Using Erlang pattern matching for compile-time security guarantees\n- Implementing fail-closed security (reject by default)\n- Maintaining backward compatibility with legitimate SSH clients\n- Enforcing SSH protocol specification requirements\n\n**The server can no
